# ARCH Domain Rows — Batch ES-2026-05-08-ARCH

Compact DPO rows for the ARCH adapter. Type A (adjacent sub-phase
confusion) is the dominant rejected pattern per the v8 ARCH confusion
table. ARCH had **zero training batches** in calibration run 2026-05-06 —
this is the highest-priority adapter to fill.

## Common coverage labels (apply to every row in this file)

```
domain_label:         ARCH
source_license_state: external_license_checked
contract_signature:   DomainTranslatorUnit + SchemePlannerUnit + ArbiterUnit
node_role:            row_level_pair
pair_kind:            flow_node
coverage_status:      covered
attribution_required: false
```

Format: `[ID | type/OR/trans/sub] sources | A | B | P | R`

ARCH confusion patterns covered (per v8 reference table):
- 3.1 vs 4.1 (boundary vs class body)
- 3.1 vs 4.4 (ownership vs refactor)
- 3.2 vs 4.1 (model vs repository)
- 3.2 vs 4.5 (boundary vs adapter)
- 3.5 vs 4.1 (signal design vs logger wiring)
- 3.5 vs 7.3 (design vs dashboard config)
- 6.1 vs 5.1 (human review vs unit test)
- 6.1 vs 5.3 (human review vs end-to-end automation)

[ARCH-0252 | D/0.16/feature_addition/3.1] arc42+ADR | A:"Document choice between event-driven vs request-response between services" | B:ADR Decision (event-driven via outbox + Kafka) | P:ADR Alternative (synchronous HTTP between services) | R:[WRONG: per ADR — coupling and cascading-failure risk]
[ARCH-0253 | D/0.17/feature_addition/3.2] arc42+ADR | A:"Document choice between SCD-Type-2 and event-sourcing for customer history" | B:ADR Decision (SCD-Type-2 with effective dates) | P:ADR Alternative (full event-sourcing) | R:[WRONG: per ADR — implementation cost not justified for read patterns]
[ARCH-0254 | D/0.19/feature_addition/3.5] dapr+ADR | A:"Document choice between push vs pull metrics architecture" | B:ADR Decision (pull via Prometheus) | P:ADR Alternative (push via StatsD) | R:[WRONG: cardinality unmanaged risk; pull lets backend cap]
[ARCH-0255 | D/0.18/feature_addition/3.4] rfc-7519+ADR | A:"Document choice between own IdP vs third-party (Auth0/Okta)" | B:ADR Decision (Okta) | P:ADR Alternative (own self-hosted IdP) | R:[WRONG: compliance burden too high]
[ARCH-0256 | D/0.16/feature_addition/3.1] arc42+ADR | A:"Document choice between Kafka vs RabbitMQ for event distribution" | B:ADR Decision (Kafka) | P:ADR Alternative (RabbitMQ) | R:[WRONG: ordering guarantees per partition required]
[ARCH-0257 | D/0.17/feature_addition/3.5] temporal+ADR | A:"Document choice between Temporal vs Step Functions for orchestration" | B:ADR Decision (Temporal — code-defined) | P:ADR Alternative (AWS Step Functions) | R:[WRONG: vendor-lock and JSON state-machine limitations]
[ARCH-0258 | D/0.18/feature_addition/3.1] arc42+ADR | A:"Document choice between sticky sessions vs distributed sessions" | B:ADR Decision (Redis-backed distributed) | P:ADR Alternative (sticky session via load-balancer) | R:[WRONG: sticky breaks on scale-out events]
[ARCH-0259 | D/0.19/feature_addition/3.4] rfc-6749+ADR | A:"Document choice between OAuth scopes vs RBAC permissions" | B:ADR Decision (RBAC at app + OAuth scopes at API) | P:ADR Alternative (OAuth scopes only) | R:[WRONG: scopes too coarse-grained for app authorisation]

[ARCH-0351 | G/0.14/repair/3.4] cwe-285 | A:"Issue: AC-3 violation on /admin/users" | B:correct fix — middleware role check + regression test | P:wrong fix — 'add admin-only flag in JWT and trust client' | R:[WRONG: client-trusted flag spoofable; server-side check required]
[ARCH-0352 | G/0.16/repair/3.4] cwe-918 | A:"Issue: SSRF via webhook-test endpoint" | B:correct fix — host allowlist after canonicalisation | P:wrong fix — 'block private IP ranges via firewall only' | R:[WRONG: DNS rebinding bypasses; canonicalise+pin needed]
[ARCH-0353 | G/0.13/repair/3.5] dapr-observability | A:"Issue: stuck workflow not detected for 4 hours" | B:correct fix — emit workflow.last_activity heartbeat metric with alert | P:wrong fix — 'increase log verbosity' | R:[WRONG: more logs ≠ detection; explicit metric needed]
[ARCH-0354 | G/0.15/repair/3.2] arc42 | A:"Issue: deleted users still in soft-deleted query results" | B:correct fix — query builder default WHERE deleted_at IS NULL | P:wrong fix — 'add manual filter to each query' | R:[WRONG: each query missed eventually; default-clause is the systemic fix]
[ARCH-0355 | G/0.14/repair/3.1] arc42+ADR | A:"Issue: order service and inventory share a database" | B:correct fix — split databases per ADR; integration via events | P:wrong fix — 'add views with row-level security' | R:[WRONG: views ratify shared coupling; ADR rejects]
[ARCH-0356 | G/0.16/repair/3.4] cwe-352 | A:"Issue: GET /admin/delete?id=X CSRF defect" | B:correct fix — POST + CSRF token | P:wrong fix — 'add Referer-header check' | R:[WRONG: Referer suppressed by proxies; token required]
[ARCH-0357 | G/0.13/repair/3.4] cwe-79 | A:"Issue: XSS via reflected query parameter" | B:correct fix — context-aware encoding at template engine | P:wrong fix — 'add input filter blocking <script>' | R:[WRONG: script tags only one vector; context-aware encoding handles all]
[ARCH-0358 | G/0.15/repair/3.5] temporal | A:"Issue: saga compensation runs even when forward did not take effect" | B:correct fix — track activity-completion; compensate only successful | P:wrong fix — 'compensate everything for symmetry' | R:[WRONG: violates Temporal saga rule; corrupts state]
[ARCH-0359 | G/0.14/repair/3.4] cwe-269 | A:"Issue: user can self-promote via PATCH /me with role field" | B:correct fix — server-side denylist of role field on self-edit | P:wrong fix — 'remove role field from UI form' | R:[WRONG: API still accepts; UI is not authoritative]
[ARCH-0360 | G/0.16/repair/3.5] dapr+nist-au-2 | A:"Issue: audit log gaps for sensitive-data access" | B:correct fix — design event taxonomy + emit at every access | P:wrong fix — 'enable trace logging for the service' | R:[WRONG: trace logs noisy + unstructured; explicit audit events required]
